# Product backlog

This is the implementation tracking source of truth. The execution strategy and
definition of done live in [`README.md`](./README.md).

## Status and priority

- `done` — completed and validated; Evidence contains a commit.
- `in progress` — the single item currently being implemented.
- `next` — first item to pull after the current checkpoint.
- `ready` — sufficiently defined and waiting on its dependencies.
- `blocked` — cannot proceed until the named dependency or approval is resolved.
- `later` — intentionally ordered behind higher-priority work.
- `deferred` — outside the initial release.

Priorities describe product value, not strict technical order:

- `P0` — required for the mobile-first release.
- `P1` — backend/reliability work or mobile completeness needed before production.
- `P2` — admin-web delivery, the lowest-priority application surface.
- `P3` — explicitly deferred work.

Decisions taken under the blanket approval: Assistants and Administrators read any Client (Clients have no Branch) and act on Bookings/Waitlist only at assigned Branches (the list flags `actionable`); a pending-activation Client may receive cash Payments and Bookings, so an invited Client with a Subscription, Payment, or Booking cannot be cancelled (`409 invitation_has_activity`) and is skipped by the 30-day expiry cleanup; search is a bounded 20-row case-insensitive contains on name/email/phone (min 2 chars, no index yet); resend throttling answers `429 email_throttled` without a retry time; history stays an unpaginated array like the Client endpoint. Smoke-tested against the local API as camila (search, overview, quotes, invite → cash → 409 cancel → 429 resend → fresh invite cancelled → 404; Trainer 403). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: block end may only be set or moved earlier (R1); snake_case codes, reusing Booking's `no_eligible_subscription`; reconcilers are worker interval sweeps like the renewal reminder (pg-boss `schedule: false`), no new queues; an end with the same resolved date is an equivalent retry, a later one `409 availability_block_ended`, an earlier one shortens; ending needs only ownership, create/replace re-check the Branch assignment and archival; a Client without an Account gets no reminder but the marker settles it; staff notices open `nutritionist_agenda`, Client ones `evaluation`; lock-screen push text and sound flags live in `evaluation-notifications.ts` for NOT-002; audit correlation id is the Evaluation id; both Client lists use the cursor page shape (20 default, 50 max); Administrator list/cancel routes wait for WEB-008 (the use case already allows Administrators); no Evaluation fixture, so month-rule specs stay deterministic. Gaps: D15 callers arrive with WEB-003/WEB-004; push delivery is NOT-002; Evaluation retention waits for BE-007; the six design follow-ups belong to MOB-016. |

Decisions taken under the blanket approval: Client entry points are the two Perfil rows (the Home quick action is MOB-019); the effective date offers today plus 14 days (the API allows any later date; a longer horizon has no screen); the time grid spans 06:00–22:00; removal always confirms, edits confirm only when bookings exist; the Client subscription gate uses `active`/`expiring_soon` and the server still decides eligibility; the Nutritionist agenda loads 50 entries (no "Ver más" yet). Decided under the blanket approval: where MOB-016 differed from the follow-ups (button labels «Aplicar cambio» / «Quitar horario», removal always confirming, the date-chip strip, the combined «Desde el … · hasta el …» line, the S15 notice body) the design follows the app; checklist 30 §8 now counts seven types on 19 and six on S20. |

Verified 2026-09-22: unit 361/362 (the known pg-boss runtime env failure), e2e 75/83 (the 8 known `app.e2e` role `test` failures), integration 33 suites/136 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, including 5 new real-PostgreSQL cases (eligible values, ineligible schedules, idempotent rerun that keeps a cancelled occurrence cancelled, horizon advance, Branch and Trainer overlap, concurrent runs), `pnpm check:api` clean, touched files lint clean (the 207 repo lint errors are pre-existing elsewhere); smoke on `pilates_dev_sim`: `node dist/src/worker-main.js` added Thursday 22 Oct's three Sessions (74 → 77, same Branch/Class Type/Trainer/capacity/50 min as 15 Oct), a second run added none, no failure record; `node dist/src/main.js` served them on `GET /sessions` and `/sessions/{id}`, Lucía got `409 no_eligible_subscription` (her Plan ends 12 Oct), Sofía booked (12 → 11 seats) and cancelled (back to 12). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: schedules at an archived Branch, with an archived Class Type, or whose Trainer lacks the Trainer Role or the Branch assignment are skipped (BKM-001 validation); an occurrence that would overlap a scheduled Session is skipped silently and counted, and older schedules (`createdAt`, then `id`) materialize first so the overlap winner is deterministic; duration comes from the Class Type at materialization time and capacity from the schedule; one statement per schedule without an explicit transaction; development fixtures still write Sessions so `db:fixtures` works without a worker and the reconciler only fills the rest. Gaps: schedule edits with an effective date (update/replace/remove unbooked future Sessions) arrive with their commands in ADM-001/WEB-003; an exception that moves a generated Session's start must keep the reconciler from recreating the original start (ADM-001); Class Type or Branch archival does not withdraw Sessions already materialized. |

Verified 2026-09-23: unit 368/369 (the known pg-boss runtime env failure), e2e 75/83 (the 8 known `app.e2e` role `test` failures), integration 34 suites/143 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, including 7 new real-PostgreSQL cases (zero-Booking cancellation at the exact due instant and not 1 ms before, with Waitlist closure and both notices and a rerun that adds nothing; a booked Session keeps its marker and is never re-checked after dropping to zero; a Session that drops to zero before the check stays bookable and is cancelled at the check; an Administrator-cancelled Session is ignored; `session_started` closure only at start, without a notice, once; a Booking landing while the check holds the Session row commits and the check retries and leaves the Session scheduled, and a mutation to `FOR UPDATE` makes that case fail; two concurrent workers cancel and notify once), `pnpm check:api` clean, touched files lint clean; smoke on `pilates_dev_sim` with three Urubó Sessions for Emma (A in 4 min, capacity 1, booked by Lucía with Valeria's Entry; B in 60 min, empty; C in 115 min, booked by Sofía): `node dist/src/worker-main.js` at start cancelled B (`zero_bookings`, no actor, marker set), set only the marker on A and C, left the 6 already-ended unchecked Sessions alone; `node dist/src/main.js` showed Emma `GET /notifications` "Tu clase de Mat Pilates de hoy 01:00 se canceló: no tuvo reservas." (`trainer_schedule`, unread 1), B `404` on `/sessions/{id}` and `403` on booking, Valeria's Waitlist join inside the window `403`; the minute sweep closed A's Entry as `session_started` at 04:04:41 (A started 04:04:00) with no notice to Valeria; no `*_failed` events. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: sweep every minute like Evaluation reminders (each check at most a minute late), empty check before Waitlist closure; the check stays due until the Session ends so one missed while the worker was down still settles an empty class in progress, while an already-ended Session is left alone; Trainer copy follows design follow-up 15 ("Tu clase de {Class Type} de hoy HH:MM se canceló: no tuvo reservas.", or "del {weekday d de month}, HH:MM" on another Bolivia day), type `session_cancelled`, category `booking_schedule`, destination `trainer_schedule` (Mi agenda) and business key `session-cancelled:{id}` shared with the Administrator cancel so one Session never notifies twice; Clients still on the Waitlist of an automatically cancelled Session get the same notice as for an Administrator cancellation (BOOK-001), and none for `session_started`; no audit record for the automatic cancellation (BOOK-001); Subscription expiry stays derived at purchase time (`isSubscriptionEffective`), so Session-start finalization writes nothing to Subscriptions. Gaps: the Staff app does not route the `trainer_schedule` destination on tap yet (MOB-017 gap); an edit that moves a Session's `starts_at` must clear the marker (ADM-001); push delivery of these notices waits for NOT-002. |

Both Promotions unpublished in the admin web («Dejar de publicar»), `GET /promotions` `[]`, the old public URL `404`; on the iPhone the mounted Home still showed both cards until a pull to refresh removed the section, leaving the plan card under the greeting. Both then deleted through the admin web dialog; rows left in `pilates_dev_sim`: `667ced58…` («Nueva sala de Reformer en Urubó») and `d4e90593…` («Clases de Barre los sábados») archived (10 archived, 0 live), their two image assets released for the worker's next sweep; no `pilates_test_*` created. The 404-between-list-and-image case is covered by the Jest spec only (it cannot be timed by hand). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: React Native's own `Image` and a snapping `ScrollView` suffice (no expo-image, which is not in the app despite the brief, and no carousel library: no dependency commit); the list query keeps Home's `refetchOnMount: "always"` with the app defaults (staleTime 0, gcTime 5 min, refetch on foreground through the focus manager, no retry) and stays out of the ADR-0035 persisted allowlist, matching the API's `private, no-cache` (ADR-0063), while the immutable public images are left to the native image cache; a Promotion whose chosen image fails to load is dropped from the carousel (a card without its image is not a Promotion) and the list is re-read once per failed URL so a re-published image with new URLs comes back; the section hides completely when the list is empty or fails (Home never breaks for the carousel); pull to refresh refreshes every Home query, not only the carousel; the section label is the export's «Novedades del estudio»; the phone card is full width (UPL-002 sized the 960/1440 variants for a 345–400 css px card) and the tablet card stops at 720 pt with the next card peeking; the dots are the export's pill style; no tap target on a card. Reuse decisions: `resolveApiUrl` extracted from the fetcher's `joinApiUrl` at its second user; the source picker is a pure module beside `home-format.ts`; the Staff app's `useIsTablet` was not moved to mobile-core because the card cap is a width rule, not a layout switch; the QR data-URI approach of `payment-screen.tsx` was not reused because the public images are unauthenticated and immutable, so a plain `Image` URL keeps the native cache. | MOB-028   | P2       | Client mobile       | Keep the pending registration across launches (continuation in SecureStore, cleared on verification, cancellation, or lapse) so «Revisa tu correo» replaces the form until the email is verified, as 01 says, and «El registro venció» can actually appear | MOB-021 | Client 01 | done | Found 2026-10-01 while building MOB-021: the pending registration lives only in memory, so the 7-day lapse frame is reachable only if the app stays open for 7 days. The backend keeps resend and correction neutral, so the app infers the lapse from its own 7-day window. **Done 2026-10-01** (red `086443c`, mobile-core `744f1bd`, Client wiring `4307829`, guide `9290742`). The row matched: `AuthenticationFlow` held the pending registration in `useState` only. New `auth/pending-registration-store.ts` (load/save/clear) and `auth/use-pending-registration.ts` (restore at launch, write-through `setPending`); `authentication-flow.tsx` takes an optional `pendingRegistrationStore`, threaded through `MobileRuntime` and `MobileNavigationProps`, and only the Client `App.tsx` creates the native one, so the Staff app and every existing spec are unchanged. **Storage decisions (blanket approval):** SecureStore, not AsyncStorage, because the continuation is a bearer secret for correction, resend, and cancellation; one key `client.pending-registration` beside the credential keys, holding JSON `{ email, pendingExpiresAt, registrationContinuation }` (the device's own 7-day window, restarted by each correction or resend as before); an unreadable value is deleted and a storage error counts as nothing kept, so a locked keychain never blocks the launch or the registration; the flow renders nothing until the load settles (as identity restoring already does) so Login never flashes; a link that opens the app skips the restore and goes to its own screen. **Clearing:** cancellation, «Registrarme de nuevo», a successful verification on this device, and any successful login (a login means there is nothing left to continue; a rejected one keeps it); a lapse is shown once («El registro venció») and cleared in the same load, so the next launch opens Login. Logout does not touch it. Checks: mobile-core Jest 34 suites / 143 (store 4, flow persistence 5), client 110, staff 66, `tsc` in all three packages. Surface: iPad Pro 11 M4 (57B2…) registered `mob028-ipad-…`, `stopApp` + relaunch → «Revisa tu correo» with the address and no Login, «Cancelar registro» → confirm → relaunch → Login; iPhone 17 Pro (7C33…) registered `mob028-iphone-…`, relaunch → «Revisa tu correo», «Volver a iniciar sesión», login as Lucía → Inicio, logout → relaunch → Login. The lapse frame was not reached on a device (it needs 7 real days); the specs cover it. Three pending registrations from the iPhone runs (two flow retries and the passing run) remain in `pilates_dev_sim` until the 7-day cleanup (soft delete only); the iPad one was cancelled. |

Verified 2026-09-23: both release-config specs 3/3, both apps typecheck; `release:smoke` passed for both (client iOS 3.49 MB / Android 3.49 MB, staff 3.45 MB / 3.45 MB) and refused a missing https URL; Maestro on iPhone 17 Pro (Expo Go, API on freshly re-applied `pilates_dev_sim`): client 3/3 and staff 4/4 passed (two first-run selector/timing flakes fixed in the flows: wait for the cancel sheet to close before tapping, tap the search field by placeholder). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: version stays in `app.json` (the operator confirms it per ADR-0038) with EAS-managed build numbers; the `appVersion` runtime policy so any native change forces a new version; API URLs come from EAS environments, never committed; release smoke is a production JS export rather than a local native build (no signing identity available). **Blocked (accounts):** EAS project IDs (`eas init`), `expo-updates` with its update URL, Apple and Google store records and credentials, a signed production build, TestFlight/internal-track install, Maestro on that build (`APP_ID=com.emmapilates.<app>`), store submission, and a first production update rollout — all need the Expo, Apple Developer, and Google Play accounts. |

Decisions taken under the blanket approval: the catalog lives in docs with an executable sync check rather than a runtime registry; activation is audited (ADR-0055 lists password setting/replacement) while ordinary self-service and system sweeps are not; staff-management codes arrive with IDN-007 and Back Office codes with BKO-001. Retention (provisional): audit records are kept indefinitely, the append-only triggers already prevent deletion, and BE-008 never sweeps this table. **Blocked sub-part (BE-007):** how long must audit records be kept (financial events under Bolivian tax obligations versus security events), and must the emails stored in `client_invitation.email_corrected`/`.cancelled` metadata be redacted on Account closure? |

Verified 2026-09-23: unit 409/410 (the known pg-boss runtime env failure), e2e 80/88 (the 8 known `app.e2e` role `test` failures; +5 staff-management), integration 37 suites/153 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (+5: create with Branch, email job, and audit then page read; Nutritionist Role change ends her blocks and withdraws future unbooked slots through the D15 port; Trainer Branch removal refused with no change; Assistant deactivation revokes sessions as `account_disabled`; a pending Administrator does not count toward the last-Administrator guard), `pnpm check:api` clean, touched files lint and typecheck clean; curl smoke on `pilates_dev_sim` as Paola: list with filters, create a two-Branch Nutritionist, immediate resend `429 email_throttled`, Branch removal, Role change, deactivation, repeated deactivation `409 staff_member_deactivated`, Emma's Branch removal `409 trainer_has_upcoming_sessions`, self-deactivation `409 own_staff_member`, Assistant `403`, anonymous `401`, bad filter `400`; four `staff_member.*` audits written. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: Staff management is a Booking & Membership use case (Staff Member, Role, Branch) that asks System Identity for the Account in the same transaction; Administrators carry no Branch rows (promotion clears them, demotion requires a Branch set); a Trainer with upcoming scheduled Sessions or still-effective Session Schedules (at the removed Branches) cannot lose them, change Role, or be deactivated until an Administrator swaps them (ADM-001), rather than silently orphaning classes; Assistants have no such guard; an Administrator cannot change or deactivate their own Staff Member, and the last activated, non-disabled Administrator cannot be demoted or deactivated; deactivation disables the Account, revokes every Authentication Session, and invalidates unused links (ADR-0030); the same-Role change is a no-op; admin lists use page-and-offset (default 20, max 100) with `role`, `branchId`, and `status` filters (ADR-0061/0062). Gaps: no reactivation or pending-invitation cancellation command yet (no screen asks for them; WEB-004 may); Branch archival (WEB-003/ADM-001) must still call the D15 port; staff email correction is not offered. |

Verified 2026-09-24: backend unit 104 suites / 506 tests, e2e 30 / 160 (`app.e2e` boots on `pilates_test_fixtures`), integration 58 / 246 on `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (the new `account-email-change` suite failed once in a full run that overlapped the e2e run, green alone and in the full rerun; the discovery and foundation specs were brought current with `photo` and the new table, `b201e1b`), `check:api` and `check:api-compat` clean, lint and `tsc` clean; client-mobile Jest 23 suites / 88 tests and `tsc`; mobile-core 26 / 102; staff-mobile 11 / 46 and `tsc`. Surface walk on `pilates_dev_sim` (API 3100 and worker rebuilt, Metro 8081): curl as Lucía: `email_unchanged` 400 for her own address, `202` and a stored pending change but no captured email for `sofia@example.com`, `202` for `Lucia.Nueva@example.com` with two files in `.data/email-capture` (`email_change` to the new address with the link, `email_change_notice` to the current one), an immediate resend `202` with no new file (60-second lane), `POST /email-changes` with the captured token → `changed` (then `expired`), the old session still read the profile as `lucia.nueva@example.com`, Login refused the old address (401) and accepted the new one (201), `account.email_changed` carried `previousEmail`, the admin clients list and detail (AW22's data) showed the current address throughout, and the change back completed the same way. iPhone 17 Pro and iPad Pro 11-inch (Expo Go, Maestro `email-change.yaml`): Editar perfil → edit the email → Guardar cambios → the card with the new address and the 24-hour line → Reenviar correo («Te enviamos otro enlace.») → Cancelar cambio → Volver shows the current address; the app's requests wrote the capture files. Decisions taken under the blanket approval beyond the proposal: the link inspection endpoint keeps answering `invalid` for an `email_change` token (the confirmation screen completes directly; nothing probes it); the confirmation screen completes without a session and never starts one; the pending card shows no countdown and no link; the field returns to the current address once the change is pending; `email_unavailable` is shown to the link holder (they control that mailbox); the notice names the new address; the placeholder renderer carries the two new purposes until EML-001. Gaps and open questions: the Simulator cannot open the link until LNK-001 (the walk pasted the captured token into the API); Staff Members have no screen for it (the command is Account-level); `previousEmail` in the audit metadata joins the BE-007 redaction question; expired change rows linger until tokens are swept (BE-008); the link screens still open through automated tests only. **Design 2026-09-30:** follow-up 64 exported (`1100c97`, README `6216349`): `22` draws the editable email, the pending card with Reenviar correo and Cancelar cambio, the neutral resend line, the malformed-email error, and the link's screens (Confirmando tu correo, then Tu correo cambió · Enlace vencido · Enlace no válido · Correo no disponible with Continuar), matching the shipped screens. |
| BE-008  | P1       | Backend              | Retention sweeps for expired idempotency records (≥ 48 h), expired/revoked Authentication Sessions, old throttle events, and read notifications      | BE-006, BE-007                  | done    | Policy red `97cdd37` → `47c803c` (`retentionCutoffs`); Prisma sweeps red `78df457` → `73e81d1` (`createPrismaRetentionSweeps`: parameterized `DELETE … WHERE id IN (SELECT … LIMIT n)` batches of 1 000, at most 50 per table per run, over existing indexes, so no migration); worker red `5f3b4c0` → `fa40f04` (at start and hourly, sanitized `retention_sweep_failed`), entrypoint `00531a8`; testing guide `588b1a3` (Test 16). Provisional windows (recorded as provisional until BE-007): idempotency records once past their own `expires_at` **and** at least 48 h old (commands set 24/48/72 h expiries, so the longer of the two wins); Authentication Sessions 90 days after revocation, idle expiry, or absolute expiry (keeps sign-in history for incident review); throttle events 30 days after `expires_at`; notifications 180 days after `read_at` (deliveries cascade; unread notifications are never swept; 180 days is far beyond any business event that could re-fire the same `business_event_key`). Never touched: audit records (append-only, BE-006), Accounts, Clients, Payments, Evaluations, receipts (their own ADR-0075 schedule). Gap: the windows are provisional; BE-007 may lengthen or shorten them (see its open questions). |

| BE-010  | P1       | Backend              | Give every date-bound integration spec an injected clock so none mixes the real clock with fixed 2026 date ranges; `booking-command` first, since every case but the reschedule one fails from 01/10/2026 | BE-009 | done    | Proof before the fix (2026-09-23): the whole integration suite on a scratch Jest config that fakes only `Date` (`fakeTimers` with `advanceTimers`, every timer in `doNotFake`) at 2026-10-15 10:00 Bolivia failed 18/201 in four specs; the same run of e2e passed 133/133. `booking-command`: red `21a6779` (`createFixture` now requires `now`; every case passes one file clock, pinned first to 2026-10-01 10:00 Bolivia: 12/13 fail with «No eligible Subscription») → green `7b325ae` (the clock moves to 2026-09-15 10:00 Bolivia, inside the 01/09–30/09 Subscriptions; the reschedule and idempotency cases keep their own clocks). `evaluation-test-support` (shared by the oversight, evaluation, back-office, catalog, and schedule-administration specs): `applyDevelopmentFixtures` planned Evaluation slots from the real clock while the world’s Subscriptions and `eligibilityMonth` are fixed to September, so `administration-oversight` failed 4/4 on `evaluations_slot_month_fkey`; red `8aebf24` (fixture clock injected at 2026-10-15) → green `dffd4bb` (`now: () => fixtureNow`). `booking-catalog-repository` «reads published Promotion metadata»: the image upload took `created_at` from the real clock against a fixed `expiresAt` of 01/10/2026 (`image_assets_valid_lifetime_check`); red `7e89cfe` (stamped 01/10) → green `dd72d81` (stamped 31/08, before the fixed display dates). `session-cancellation`: fixtures pinned to 21/09 with a real-clock Session filter; red `662e832` (clock 2026-10-15: «No eligible Subscription») → green `d3e4cc6`. Decision: this one is made fully relative (`today = boliviaDate(now)` with the real clock) instead of fixed, because Session cancellation stamps Bookings’ `cancelled_at` from the command clock while Prisma stamps their `created_at` from the real one, and `cancelled_at >= created_at` is enforced, so a fixed past clock cannot pass. Checked and left alone (already fixed or fully relative, or their real-clock values never meet a fixed range): `booking-catalog-concurrency`, `client-invitations`, `development-fixtures`, `upload-idempotency`, every other integration spec, and all e2e specs. No `pilates_test_*` databases left behind. Gap: the unit suite under the same global fake-`Date` config fails the worker and heartbeat specs only because they install their own fake timers (on the real clock only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec` fails); a committed guard that runs the suites on a shifted clock is not added. |

Decisions taken under the blanket approval: the receipt route keeps one URL and chooses the variant by query (default `readable`) so no client changes; profile photos are centre-cropped squares until an app screen supplies framing; WebP for Promotions, JPEG for receipts and profile photos; a Promotion image must be at least 1200×675 (AW18) and is centre-cropped to 16:9; delete archives (BKM-001) and releases the image; replacing or removing the image of a published Promotion unpublishes it (follow-up 39) and the old image's variants are released at once (ADR-0076); publishing copies the private variants to versioned public keys and re-publishing makes new ones; only the acting Administrator's own `promotion` uploads may be attached; create needs `Idempotency-Key`, the other commands address one identified transition; Promotion changes are audited except reordering; the local API serves the public images in place of the R2 origin (a `publicImageRoute` builder is the seam for EXT-002); no schema change was needed (BKM-001's `promotions` and UPL-001's `image_variants` already had every column). Reuse decisions: `runIdempotent` moved to `src/database` at its second user with an `onKeyReused` callback; the idempotency header shape and parser moved to `src/http` (Back Office, payments, and evaluations keep their own copies for now); `requireAdministrator` and `parsed`/`requireId` reused from `booking/administration`; payments' inline receipt attach was left in place (moving it onto `attachImageAsset` is a later refactor). Open questions for the user (data governance and product, not decided): how long archived Promotions, their copy, and their audit history are kept (BE-007); what a Promotion may show (purely informational per the glossary, but no content rule beyond the 80/280 characters is enforced); whether re-publishing should also require a display window that has not ended. Gaps: the review DTO's `receipt.mediaType` still names the upload's format (the caption reads «PNG» while the served variant is JPEG); WEB-006 may switch AW29's panel to `?variant=thumbnail`; the admin list has no filename for AW18's «filename + dimensions»; fixtures ship no Promotion (an image object cannot be seeded by the fixtures command); the public list is not paged and the reorder body caps at 200 ids; ended Promotions keep their public copies until unpublished or archived (the sweep does not watch the window); the Client app's receipt preview keeps its local file, as required; the iOS Simulator panel could not attach (`xcode-select` points elsewhere), so the Staff check ran through Expo Go and Maestro on the iPhone 17 Pro; no iPad run (the Staff review screen did not change). | BKO-001 | P2       | Backend design       | Propose the Back Office schema for Supplies, Withdrawals, Salary Payments, Expenses, and Expense Categories                                         | Back Office glossary            | done    | Proposal `f667e78` ([BKO-001 proposal](./BKO-001-back-office-schema-proposal.md)) and migration review package `cc80aeb` ([review](./BKO-001-migration-review.md), [Prisma](./prisma/back-office.prisma), [generated SQL](./sql/back-office-review.sql), [protections](./sql/back-office-constraints.sql), [rejection cases](./sql/back-office-review-test.sql)), each committed alone; no migration written. Proposed `20260924000000_back_office`: creates the `back_office` schema and six tables (`supplies`, `withdrawals`, `supply_restocks`, `salary_payments`, `expense_categories`, `expenses`) with restrictive foreign keys to `booking.branches`, `booking.staff_members`, and `system.accounts`; no existing table changes. Verified 2026-09-23 on disposable databases (dropped): every shipped migration plus `prisma validate` on a merged copy; the generated SQL is `prisma migrate diff` output minus the ten known drift lines, and after applying it with the protections the diff shows only that drift; the review test passes all 31 expected failures plus a stock reconciliation check; two concurrent 6-of-10 Withdrawals left 4 units and one row. Decisions under the blanket approval (D1–D20): Administrator only for every command and read (recorder Account kept, so Assistants later need no migration); whole-unit stock kept by the database (`initial_quantity` fixed, `quantity_on_hand` changed only by Withdrawal/Restock insert triggers under the Supply row lock, never negative, always initial + restocks − withdrawals); **new Restock movement** (units in, no money) because nothing else can raise stock and AW09 forbids editing it; Withdrawals and Restocks immutable, no void; movement time is the recording instant, month by Bolivia month; low stock derived as at-or-below the threshold, badge only; Supply name and threshold editable, unique active name per Branch (`lower(btrim())` like the catalog), one-way archive instead of delete; Salary Payment editable (Staff Member of any Role, deactivated included, amount, `paid_on`); Expense Branch immutable, category/amount/`incurred_on` editable; amounts `> 0`; categories create/delete only, delete refused while referenced; archived Branches take no new records but are not blocked from archival; Read Committed (the row lock makes the stock change atomic, superseding the full release note's Serializable); Idempotency-Key on the four money/stock POSTs; `salary_payment.*`/`expense.*` recorded/updated audited (catalog rows added by BKO-002 with the writes), stock and categories not; no check compares a `now()` default with an application instant (the ADM-001a lesson). Open for the user (provisional answers built into the package): **Q1** may Salary Payments/Expenses be deleted or voided (provisional: no, edits audited); **Q2** future dates or locked months (provisional: up to today, no lock); **Q3** Salary Payments in a single-Branch AW16 report (provisional: all-Branches only, like transfer income); **Q4** retention and personal data (BE-007; provisional: keep indefinitely). Required with BKO-002: glossary entry for Restock and the at-or-below wording; design follow-ups for AW08 (Registrar reposición), AW09 (Archivar insumo), AW10 (insufficient stock), and AW16 per Q3. Known limitation: non-ASCII case folding in name uniqueness depends on the database locale, as for Plans and Class Types. |

Verified 2026-09-23: unit 96/97 suites, 419/420 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`); e2e 20/20 suites, 101/101 tests (+6 Back Office; with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures` the `app.e2e` cases pass too); integration 46/46 suites, 187/187 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (+5 suites, +18 tests: schema rejection cases, stock kept by the database, idempotent replay and key reuse, two concurrent Withdrawals where exactly one takes the last units, concurrent same-key replay, Bolivia month boundaries at 23:30/00:30, audits in the same transaction, report with and without a Branch); typecheck clean; `pnpm check:api` clean; the 20 touched TypeScript files lint and Prettier clean. Curl smoke on `pilates_dev_sim` as Paola: Supply create, Withdrawal 3 (7 left), exact replay (same id), reused key `409 idempotency_key_reused`, 50 units `409 insufficient_stock`, missing key `400`, Withdrawal 4, Restock 12 (15), AW31 month list with totals 7, category create, Expense Bs 450.50, category delete `409 expense_category_in_use`, Salary Payment Bs 3 500, future date `400 date_in_future`, report all Branches 395 050 and Centro 45 050 without payroll, anonymous `401`, Camila `403 back_office_access_denied`; one `expense.recorded` and one `salary_payment.recorded` audit row; API stopped. Decisions under the blanket approval: `Idempotency-Key` requests take a transaction-scoped advisory lock on the key so a concurrent same-key request always replays the first response instead of failing on stock (Read Committed, no Serializable, as D15 decided); replayed responses are the stored original, with dates rebuilt; the archive route is `POST …/{id}/archive` to match ADM-001's Branch and Class Type routes (the proposal sketched `/archival`); edits to an archived Supply answer `409 supply_archived`; the expense report takes the same `from`/`until` instants as `reports/income` and reads them as Bolivia civil dates; list date filters `from`/`to` are inclusive civil dates; Back Office rows are **not** added to the development fixtures, because the fixture world also feeds every integration spec and an empty Back Office is a valid start (records come from the API). Gaps: WEB-009 still has to combine `reports/income` and `reports/expenses` into AW16's net figure; design follow-ups 48–51 await the design export; the ADM-001a gap (other adapters leaving `created_at` to the database) is unchanged. |

## External accounts and provider gates

Work that needs an account, credential, domain, or real business data the
user must provide. Local stand-ins keep development moving; none of them may
reach production.

| Account or input | Needed for | Backlog items | What must be set up | Local stand-in today |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Expo / EAS account | Push tokens, EAS Build, Submit, and Updates | NOT-002, REL-001 | Organization, project IDs for both apps, push credentials, update channels | Expo Go and dev clients; in-app inbox only |
| Apple Developer Program | iOS builds, TestFlight, APNs, Universal Links | REL-001, NOT-002, LNK-001 | Team, bundle IDs `com.emmapilates.client` and `com.emmapilates.staff`, APNs key, associated domains | iOS Simulator |
| Google Play Console + Firebase (FCM) | Android builds, store release, push | REL-001, NOT-002, LNK-001 | App entries, signing key, FCM server credentials, `assetlinks.json` fingerprint | Android not verified yet |
| SendPulse | Real authentication email | EXT-001, EML-001 | Sender domain with SPF, DKIM, DMARC; API credentials; bounce/complaint webhook | `LocalEmailCapture` in the worker |
| Cloudflare R2 | Receipt, profile, and Promotion image storage | EXT-002, UPL-002 | Private and public buckets, scoped keys, public custom domain for Promotions | `LocalObjectStorage` under `.data/uploads` |
| Northflank + Aiven PostgreSQL | Production API, worker, migrations, database | EXT-003 | Services, secrets store, backups, staged migration job | Local API, worker, and PostgreSQL |
| Render + Neon | Shared development API and database for previews | EXT-003 | Services and development secrets | `pilates_dev_sim` locally |
| Vercel | Admin-web previews and production | EXT-003, WEB-010 | Project, protected previews, `/api/v1` proxy | Vite dev server |
| GitHub Actions | CI and deployments | EXT-003, BE-003 | Workflows, disposable PostgreSQL, deployment-only credentials | Local test runs |
| Better Stack | Error reporting, uptime, and heartbeat alerts | BE-005 gap, EXT-003 | Sources, monitors, email alerts | JSON logs on stdout |
| Domain and DNS | API host, admin host, mobile-link domain, email sending domain | LNK-001, EXT-001, EXT-003 | Registered domain and records | `localhost` and custom URL schemes |
| Studio business data | Transfer instructions and QR shown to Clients | Production config | Real `TRANSFER_BENEFICIARY_NAME`, `TRANSFER_BANK_NAME`, `TRANSFER_ACCOUNT_NUMBER`, QR image | Placeholder values in `.env.example` |
| Production secrets | Link encryption and email digests | EXT-003 | Fresh `AUTHENTICATION_EMAIL_DIGEST_KEY`, `AUTHENTICATION_LINK_ENCRYPTION_KEY(_ID)` with a rotation plan, `RELEASE_ID` per deploy | Development-only keys |

## Production readiness checklist

Nothing reaches real users until each line is done:

- [x] Sessions materialize without fixtures (BKM-003) and empty Sessions cancel themselves (BOOK-004).
- [x] First Administrator bootstrap command and runbook (IDN-006); a production run still needs real email (EXT-001).
- [ ] Real email templates and SendPulse delivery (EML-001, EXT-001); R2 storage (EXT-002; the variants exist since UPL-002).
- [ ] Push delivery and store builds (NOT-002, REL-001) with Universal/App Links (LNK-001).
- [ ] Hosting, CI, secrets, staged migrations, and monitoring (EXT-003, BE-005 gaps, BE-003).
- [ ] Data governance: Account closure, personal-data and Evaluation retention, audit retention, backups, restore drills, incident runbooks (BE-006, BE-007, BE-008).
- [ ] Staff management and admin web for studio operation (IDN-007, ADM-001, BKO-001/002, WEB-001–WEB-010).
- [ ] Real transfer instructions and production secrets configured (see the gate table above).
- [ ] The two local-only test failures pass in CI (`app.e2e-spec.ts` needs a PostgreSQL role for the app module; `pg-boss-runtime.spec.ts` needs `--experimental-vm-modules`).

## Admin-web queue

Admin web is the lowest-priority application surface. It is still necessary for
full studio operation before production; local fixtures temporarily provide the
catalog and schedule data needed to build mobile first.

Decisions taken under the blanket approval: one browser Login lane shared with mobile (same email/client/network throttle rules), with the client signal from the `__Host-emma-admin-client` cookie; `__Host-` cookie names so the browser enforces host-only and Secure; Max-Age equals the 12 h absolute lifetime (the server still enforces idle); only unsafe methods are origin-checked (GET is safe and SameSite=Lax), a missing Origin is refused, and bearer requests from the apps are never origin-checked; the cookie reaches other contexts' endpoints as an internal `AdminSession` credential rather than a new guard on 168 handlers; `GET …/current` answers 401 once the Staff Member is no longer an Administrator (demotion ends browser access); logout always clears the cookie and answers 204 even if the session had already ended; a non-Administrator who completes a recovery link in the browser gets the password replaced and every session revoked but no browser session (`signedIn: false`), because the link proves mailbox control and recovery is Account-wide (ADR-0030); link inspection keeps one expired state for expired and already used links (the backend does not distinguish them); one AW32 route `/crear-contrasena?token=` for both link purposes, chosen by the inspected purpose (the email URL shape is EML-001's); an unknown address shows Página no encontrada without the shell or the guard; activation, recovery request, inspection, and password change reuse their existing operations, so only four new admin-web-only operations exist; the admin menu labels the Role «Administración» like the Staff app's S23 instead of AW33's «Administradora» (follow-up 52); client passwords need at least 8 characters, the mobile rule; untrusted-origin refusals and browser Login/logout are not audited (catalog). Gaps: the real Vercel proxy (forwarded client address, `trust proxy`, preview-origin registration) stays in WEB-010/EXT-003; emails still carry the placeholder `pilates://` link, so an Administrator's link needs EML-001 to point at `/crear-contrasena`; valid activation and recovery links cannot be read from the local email capture, so a successful activation or reset in the browser is covered by automated tests only; an expired invitation link cannot yet be told apart from one removed by the 30-day cleanup (follow-up 35 asks for both); Safari may refuse `Secure` cookies on `http://localhost`; the bundle passed Vite's 500 kB hint, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; no Log out all other devices action yet (ADR-0030) and background polling does not exist yet to exempt from idle extension; Playwright stays a scratch script until WEB-010 adds the committed suite. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: page-and-offset catalog reads with a status or visibility filter instead of client-side paging over arrays (ADR-0061/0062); the Horario grid reads Sessions, not schedules, and shows only scheduled ones (a cancelled occurrence disappears; the week cap is the backend's 100, flagged on screen when reached); the week lives in the URL (`?week=`, Monday), the narrow day tab and open panels in local state; Trainers come from `listStaffMembers?role=trainer&branchId=` without deactivated ones; «Cancelar sesión» appears only when the Session has Bookings, because the backend refuses an empty one and the 2-hour check cancels it (follow-up 53 §1); a Session's Class Type is read-only on edit (ADM-001 C4); «Esta y las futuras» keeps the schedule's Class Type, defaults Vigente desde to the occurrence date, and never offers a date before the backend's rule (today and after the schedule's start); ending a schedule lives under «Esta y las futuras» as «Terminar la serie» (follow-up 53 §3); mutations invalidate exactly the selected Branch's week keys (a predicate on `branchId`) and its schedules, catalog changes every list of that catalog (asserted by `schedule-invalidation.spec.tsx`); Plans get «Orden en la app» and «Ocultar/Mostrar en la app», Branches and Class Types an Estado filter and an archive action (follow-up 53 §5–6); the field label stays «Entrenador» as AW03 draws it. Reuse decisions: admin web writes its own `formatBolivianos` and Bolivia-time helpers because `@pilates/mobile-core` is a React Native package and its formatter drops the decimals ADR-0082 and the design system show; a shared `CatalogPage` layout was extracted when the second catalog page appeared and Sucursales moved onto it; `ChoiceChips` is shared by the frequency and scope choices; the API-002 query was extended rather than duplicated. Gaps: native date and time pickers follow the browser's language (an English Chrome shows `09/29/2026` and `09:00 AM`), so dd/mm/aaaa and 24-hour pickers need custom controls or a Spanish browser; cancelled Sessions are not drawn (AW02 shows a «Cancelada» legend); the bundle is 675 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; the `booking-command` reschedule spec needs a fixed clock (its own item); dev fixtures reapplied do not remove Sessions or schedules created by hand. Admin web: AW21 red `63fdaae` → `5f15074` (route `/back-office/clientes` with `?q=` validated in the URL, debounced `SearchFilter`, read-only list, feminine counters «6 clientas · 5 con plan activo» and «Mostrando 6 de 6 clientas» (follow-up 28), follow-up 25's no-results message inside the table body under the kept header with no call to action and disabled paging, narrow cards); AW22 red `5e9c4e5` → `23376ba` (read-only panel, full screen when narrow: «Cliente desde», contact, Plan with dates and «5 de 8», Credit `Bs 1.050,00`, referral count and code, «Último plan vencido el dd/mm/aaaa.», «Pendiente de activación» for a pending Account, loading, `client_not_found`, and retry states); AW23 red `cfbc8f9` → `55ac62e` (route `/back-office/personal?role=`, «Todos» default, Role as a neutral tag, Branch names from every Branch with «Todas» for Administrators, Activo/Pendiente/Desactivado pills), counter `d4eba2e` → `c0528f5`; refactor `c3fb70b` (`PageNotice` shared by Horario and the list pages); AW24 create red `8ef735f` → `bb1073d` (Zod form, required email and optional phone (follow-up 33), 2×2 Role choice, Branch chips or the read-only «Todas las sucursales (implícito por el rol)» row, activation-email helper and page notice with no password (follow-up 35), `email_in_use` on its field, `email_throttled` in the panel); AW24 edit red `6f2f4d8` → `c1f14cc` (contact read-only, Role change sent with the Branches in one `PUT …/role`, Branch change through `PUT …/branches`, no request when nothing changed, the «no se reasignan solas» warning, `trainer_has_upcoming_sessions`, `last_administrator`, `own_staff_member` and the other IDN-007 codes by message, pending box with expiry, 30-day note, and «Reenviar enlace», confirmed «Desactivar cuenta», read-only own and deactivated accounts); browser fixes `161cc8e`; design follow-up 54 `f99140c`; testing guide `7f8d23b` (Test 26). Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo): Clientes listed 6 Clients (one, «Ana Nueva», is an earlier session's leftover) with «Sin plan» for Mariana; «guzman» found Sofía Guzmán and put `?q=guzman` in the address; «rodrigues» showed the three-line no-results body under the header with «Mostrando 0 de 6 clientas»; Lucía's panel showed 13/09/2026 – 13/10/2026 and «5 de 8», Mariana's «Sin plan activo»; Personal showed 8 members with neutral Role tags and «Todas» for Paola; the Entrenador filter put `?role=trainer` in the address; empty submit marked Nombre, Correo, and Rol; Administrador swapped the chips for the read-only row; a test Assistant was created («Invitación enviada a …»), its immediate resend answered the too-soon message (`429`), its Role changed to Entrenador, and it was deactivated after the confirmation; removing Urubó from Emma Vargas was refused with the Horario explanation (`409`); Paola's own row opened read-only; Horario's Trainer list for Centro stayed Daniela Ortiz and Emma Vargas (the deactivated test member excluded); at 420 px cards, the pinned search, and full-screen panels. Fixed from the walk: the browser's blue search clear button (off-palette) is hidden, the Personal counter counts «miembros», and the read-only contact labels no longer wrap. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probe and the expected `429` and `409`. The walk's two test Staff Members and their Accounts and tokens were deleted from `pilates_dev_sim`; their six append-only `staff_member.*` audit records stay, and their queued activation-email jobs (no worker ran) will find no Account. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: a new Administrator-only directory and detail instead of widening the Assistant search (bounded to 20, open to Assistants) or the overview (which carries Bookings and history AW22 does not show); search is the only filter, sent as `q`, 1–100 characters; the directory counts only Clients with an Account, like the Assistant search; `subscriptionStatus` is `none` for a Client who never bought a Plan, shown as a neutral «Sin plan» pill because «Expirado» would be false (follow-up 54 §1); the footer reads rows on the page over every Client, as follow-ups 25 and 28 draw it; `referredClientCount` counts every Referral made, pending or rewarded; a pending Account shows only its state, because the Activation Link belongs to System Identity and is managed from the Staff app; AW23's name search is not built (the backend has none and the Role filter covers a handful of people); the staff panel opens from local state like WEB-003's panels; name, email, and phone are read-only after creation because no command edits them; a Role change carries the Branches in the same command, a Branch-only change uses the Branch command, and an unchanged form sends nothing; the Branch warning is written to match the backend, which refuses rather than silently orphaning a Trainer's Sessions; «Restablecer contraseña» is not offered (ADR-0029 has no staff-assisted recovery; follow-up 54 §4); staff mutations invalidate only the `/administrator/staff-members` list prefix, which covers the Personal table and Horario's `branchTrainersQueryOptions`, never Branches or Sessions (asserted in `staff-create.spec.tsx` and `staff-edit.spec.tsx`); the Clientes page has no mutations. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` now serves read-only lists (optional create action, title from the row, counter wording, page notice) instead of a second list layout; `DataTable` draws its empty message inside the table body for every list; `ChoiceChips` gained a layout class and a `MultiChoiceChips` sibling; `branchTableQueryOptions` and `activeBranchesQueryOptions` are reused for Branch names and the picker; `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`, and `boliviaParts` for money and dates. Open questions for the user (data governance, not decided): may an Administrator see every Client's email, phone, Credit, and referral code in AW22, or should some be masked, and should reading a Client's detail be audited (reads are not audited today, ADR-0055)? How long are deactivated Staff Members, their Accounts, and invitations that expired kept (BE-007)? Gaps: pending-invitation email correction and cancellation (follow-up 35) and reactivation have no command yet; the staff resend throttle answers without `Retry-After`, so no retry time is shown (follow-up 38 asks for the server's); archived Branches still assigned to a member cannot be re-saved (`invalid_branch_assignment`); valid activation links still cannot be opened from the local email capture; the bundle grew to 699 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010. Open for the user: follow-up 54 (with 33–38, 39, 40, 52, 53 still pending export) is ready to send to the design session. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 54 is in AW21–AW24; AW24 keeps DES-001's pending frames (Cambiar correo, Cancelar invitación) over 54's simpler box, «Restablecer contraseña» is gone, AW22 keeps 66's «Cerrar cuenta» (not on the deactivated frame), and the clipped AW24/AW22 side panels were fitted. |

Curl on `pilates_dev_sim`: Paola 200 with `Cache-Control: no-store`, Camila (Assistant) 403, anonymous 401, `status=completed` 400, and the Staff app's `/payments/pending` still 200. Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` and `node dist/src/worker-main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo), with transfers raised through the Client API (upload, worker verification, Intent, receipt, submit) using a rendered sample bank receipt: Pagos showed Mariana Peña `Bs 560,00` and a throwaway Client `Bs 1.050,00` as Pendiente at `23/09/2026 · 22:51` in both zones, the badge «2», and Sofía's earlier Payment as «Bs 500,00 · con Bs 60,00 de crédito» Confirmado under Todos; the review panel loaded the receipt image; «Ver en grande» opened the viewer above the panel, and a typed rejection reason survived closing it with the scrim; rejecting Mariana showed the notice and the badge fell to 1; a second Mariana transfer rejected through the Staff app's bearer API while its panel was open made «Confirmar pago» answer `409` and the panel show «Otra persona ya decidió este pago.» with Rechazado; confirming the throwaway Client closed the panel, and Clientes, cached before as «Sin plan», showed «Mensual 16 clases · Activo»; at 420 px cards without horizontal scroll, the drawer badge, the full-screen panel, the full-screen receipt sheet with the ✕ top right, the confirmation dialog, the 301-character reason refused on its field, and «El comprobante ya no está disponible.» after marking that receipt deleted (restored afterwards). Fixed from the walk: a settled rejection now names its reason (`072b701`). A washed-out «Confirmar rechazo» in one screenshot was the button's color transition mid-animation; it read enabled 600 ms later and in the DOM. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probes and the expected `409`. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (payments, their Intents, Subscriptions, and the Credit ledger are append-only, and the ledger trigger refuses even a cascaded delete, so the cleanup transaction rolled back whole): Mariana Peña's two rejected transfers and one pending transfer (Bs 560, ready for Test 27), the throwaway Client «Camila Web Cinco» (`web005.camila@example.com`) with her confirmed transfer and Mensual 16 clases Subscription, their receipt images, notifications, and three append-only `payment.*` audit records. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: AW28 lists transfers only, because cash and Credit-only Payments are confirmed when recorded, cannot be rejected, and never wait for review (booking CONTEXT, Payment; ADR-0006; AW28 «every transfer Payment»; AW29 «solo llegan transferencias»); a new paged admin-web operation instead of widening the Staff app's pending-only array (ADR-0061/0062, ADR-0016); the backend's `status` defaults to `all` while the screen opens on Pendiente (AW28), and in Todos pending rows come first, oldest first, then decided ones newest decision first; `pendingCount` rides on every page so the subtitle and the sidebar badge need no second endpoint; the list refuses non-Administrators with `administration_access_denied` like the other admin-web oversight reads, while the Staff app's review endpoints keep `booking_access_denied` (the admin web only branches on 403); the receipt is shown from the existing authenticated, `no-store` `…/receipt` route through the same-origin proxy and the host-only session cookie rather than a new five-minute signed URL: ADR-0071's concern is a bearer URL that leaks, and this route is not a credential by itself (an ended session or a deleted receipt shows «No pudimos mostrar el comprobante.» and Reintentar re-reads the review, which the session guard turns into Login); the image never enters the query cache; «Confirmar pago» asks first because it starts the Subscription and cannot be undone, while «Rechazar» keeps AW29's inline reason with «Confirmar rechazo» as its explicit second step (follow-up 55 §4); a conflict re-reads the review and tells a Payment decided elsewhere from one refused because the Client already holds an active Plan by the review's state, since both answer `payment_confirmation_conflict`; confirming never extends an active Subscription (the backend refuses it, unchanged); a decision refreshes every Payment list and that review, plus the Clientes directory and every Client detail after a confirmation (her Plan starts, and a first Payment may award Credit to a referrer the admin web cannot name) or only her detail after a rejection (reserved Credit released), and a conflict refreshes like a confirmation (asserted in `payment-decision-cache.spec.ts`); the review has `staleTime: 0` so each opening is current; the open panel stays in local state like WEB-003/004. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained `canOpen` and `panelDescription` (and `DataTable` `canClickRow`) instead of a payments-only list layout; `ConfirmDialog` gained a primary tone; AW22's summary rows were extracted for AW29; `SearchFilter`, `ListFilter`, `StatusPill`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `SidePanel`, `formatBolivianos`, `boliviaParts`/`formatCivilDate`, `initials`, and the form primitives are reused; the receipt viewer follows the Staff app's behavior (thumbnail, «Ampliar»/«Ver en grande», full screen, no actions in the viewer) but not its code, because the Staff app fetches bytes into a data URI for React Native while the browser can load the authenticated route directly. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): what a rejection reason may contain, given the Client reads it verbatim in her app and notification (for example no bank details or other Clients' data) and whether it needs a list of standard reasons; whether reading a receipt or the Payment list should be audited (ordinary reads are not, ADR-0055); whether a decided receipt should stay viewable in the admin web for its 7-day post-decision window (today only pending rows open, as AW28 draws); how long rejected Payments and their reasons are kept (BE-007). Gaps: the sidebar count refreshes on this browser's decisions and page loads, not live when the Staff app decides (no background polling exists); the receipt route has no signed-URL variant for a future CDN; narrow rows use the shared labelled-card reflow rather than AW28's compact «Plan · fecha» card; the bundle grew to 715 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; valid activation links still cannot be opened from the local email capture. Open for the user: follow-up 55 (with 33–40 and 52–54 still pending export) is ready to send to the design session. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 55 is in AW28/AW29 (Credit line, empty and no-results frames, price and Credit rows, confirmation before «Confirmar pago», decided and receipt-unavailable states, «tú» in both notes). |

Test 32C with curl while the first card was Activa: `GET /api/v1/promotions` as Lucía `200` with `private, no-cache`, one Promotion with three WebP sources (480 × 270, 960 × 540, and 1200 × 810), the public 1440 copy fetched without a credential with `public, max-age=31536000, immutable` and `nosniff`; anonymous `401` and Lucía `403` on the Administrator routes. Rows left in `pilates_dev_sim`: the walk's two Promotions are archived (8 archived in total, 0 live), their six image assets are `deleted` and every promotion variant swept after a worker restart (empty asset folders remain under `.data/uploads`), and UPL-002's three failed `promotion` uploads still wait for the 24-hour cleanup. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: AW17 is an unpaged card list, not a `CatalogPage` table, with no search and no Branch scope; the state pill tones are Borrador neutral, Programada Advertencia, Activa Éxito, Finalizada Error (like «Venció» in Mi plan); the order on screen is the saved one until a drop, a drop persists the whole list and the answer replaces the cache (no optimistic renumbering, so no equal positions ever show), a refused drop restores the saved order with a role=alert line; keyboard reordering is the accessible route the handle must offer; the thumbnail and the 1440 px preview are plain `<img>` elements on the private route with the session cookie, never in the query cache, with the asset id appended to the URL; «Publicar» disabled until every field exists (follow-up 39) while «Guardar borrador» always saves; a published Promotion's footer is «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar» and only an image change turns it into the draft pair; «Publicar» on a new Promotion creates then publishes, and if the publish step fails the panel keeps the created row so a retry publishes instead of creating twice; delete asks first and archives (BKM-001); the image is checked in the browser (format, weight, decoded size) before the three calls, and a refused file never leaves the browser; the worker's `failed` verdict shows a generic message because the status route names no reason; the same `Idempotency-Key` per typed body for create, a fresh UUID per finalize; the window line and the image-change warning use the typed dates and the saved row, not a re-read; no drag library: pointer events and the existing primitives suffice (about 200 lines, no dependency commit). Reuse decisions: the browser upload stays in the admin web (`api/upload-image.ts`) rather than a shared package: the Client app's `receipt-upload.ts` reads files, hashes, and picks images through Expo and ends by attaching a receipt, so only the four-call order (about 25 lines) is common, and the next user is the mobile profile photo (Client 22), which would be Expo again; extract a runtime-neutral protocol package at that third user if the order changes; `useIdempotencyKey`/`idempotencyHeaders`, `ConfirmDialog`, `SidePanel`, `PageHeader`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `StatusPill`, `formatCivilDate`/`boliviaToday`/`addDays`, `civilDateField` (added beside `pastCivilDateField`), the form primitives, and `closeButtonClass` are reused; `optionalSearch` was added to `components/table/pagination.ts` for every list route. Backend proposals (not done, no migration): expose the worker's reason on `UploadStatusDto` (`failureReason`) so the admin web can say `image_dimensions_too_small` / `image_animated_not_allowed` instead of the generic line; keep the original filename on the image asset (or accept «Imagen actual» in AW18, follow-up 61 §3); the `promotion_1440` variant of a source narrower than 1440 px loses 16:9 (sharp's `withoutEnlargement` with `cover` made 1200 × 810 from a 1200 × 1600 file, while 480 and 960 were exact): size the box from the source width in `uploads/image-variants.ts`; UPL-002's review DTO `mediaType` gap stands. Open questions for the user (data governance and product, not decided): how long archived Promotions and their audit history are kept (BE-007, UPL-002's question stands); what a Promotion may show beyond the 80/280 characters; whether a Promotion whose window ended should be re-publishable without a new window (the API allows it); whether reordering should be audited (UPL-002 left it unaudited). Gaps: the mid-drag visual (tilt, dimming, insertion line) was verified in Playwright, not screenshotted in the walk; the worker's refusal reason is generic until the status route carries it; the file picker filters formats through `accept`, so only a dropped file reaches the format refusal; Firefox and WebKit journeys still need `playwright install`; the Client carousel (MOB-019) is still not built. **Design follow-up 39 exported 2026-09-30** (`76a6f95`, status `5623d3b`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas (no TurboTenant design system), read back and copied byte for byte, rendered over a scratch copy of `design/` and checked against checklist 30 before the copy; the canvas was deleted. The design now draws what shipped: AW03 «Una sola vez» / «Cada semana» with «Día de la semana» and «Vigente desde» (generates 30 days ahead), «Solo esta sesión» / «Esta y las futuras» on a series Session, a one-off Session without the choice; AW07's required «Descripción»; AW17 cards with Borrador / Programada / Activa / Finalizada; AW18 «Guardar borrador» / «Publicar» (disabled until complete), «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar», and the image-change warning; purely informational sample Promotions on AW17, AW18, and 02; AW04 with the six catalogue plans. Decided under the blanket approval: no «Archivada» card (a deleted Promotion is archived and leaves the list, as built); «Vigente desde» may be today, as built. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 61 is in AW17/AW18. |

Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo), in Centro and Equipetrol: empty-submit validation on AW09; three Centro Supplies created («Alcohol en gel» at its threshold turned the sidebar count to 1); «toallas de papel» refused as taken on its field; a Withdrawal of 7 over a stock of 6 marked «Solo hay 6 unid. en stock.» with «Registrar» disabled, then 2 recorded («quedan 4 unid.»); with the panel open on 4 units a concurrent Withdrawal of 3 through the API (201), then «Registrar» answered `409` and the panel showed «Solo hay 1 unid. en stock.» and «(1 unid.)»; a Restock of 12 («ahora hay 13 unid.»); a threshold edit cleared the badge; «Cera para piso» archived after the confirmation, left Insumos, and appeared in AW31's filter as «(archivado)»; AW31 for September listed Toallas and the earlier BKO-002 Withdrawals with «Consumo del mes», filtered to Toallas, and August showed the empty state; Equipetrol started empty and its «Jabón líquido» showed only there with its own count; Nómina with the studio-wide note, a tomorrow date refused, Emma Vargas paid Bs 3.500,00 and corrected to Bs 3.600,00, filtered in the address; Gastos in Centro with «Limpieza profunda» added inline by Enter and chosen, Bs 450,50 recorded and corrected to Bs 480,00, filtered, Equipetrol empty; Categorías with the studio-wide note, «Limpieza profunda» and «Reparación» «En uso», «Temporal» added, «temporal» refused, «Temporal» deleted after the confirmation; at 420 px the drawer count, the bottom-sheet selector, card tables with no horizontal scroll, full-screen panels, a Withdrawal over the stock refused and one of 1 recorded, a Restock of 6, the history cards, and the inline row. Fixed from the walk: `6f69906`, `1493bcc`, `a9bc287`, `66a170d`. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probes and the expected `409`s. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (the schema refuses deleting Supplies, Salary Payments, and Expenses and updating or deleting movements): Centro's «Toallas de papel», «Alcohol en gel», and archived «Cera para piso», Equipetrol's «Jabón líquido», three Withdrawals (Toallas 2 and 3, Alcohol 1), two Restocks (Toallas 12, Alcohol 6), Emma Vargas's Bs 3.600,00 Salary Payment of 24/09/2026, the «Limpieza profunda» category and its Bs 480,00 Centro Expense, and their four append-only `salary_payment.*`/`expense.*` audit records; «Temporal» was deleted. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: **Categorías de gasto is studio-wide** (BKO-001 D13: one set of labels for every Branch), so AW15 has no Branch column, the selector shows its studio-wide note there, and follow-up 56 §1 asks the design system and AW34 to say so; the Supply list became page-and-offset with a status filter rather than client-side paging over the array (ADR-0061/0062, as WEB-003 did), and the low-stock count rides on every page so the sidebar needs no second endpoint; the sidebar count and both movement panels read the same one-page-of-100 active list, so a movement refreshes both at once; AW08 shows only active Supplies and AW31's filter every Supply including archived ones; a Withdrawal or Supply rename/archive refreshes that Branch's Supply lists and withdrawal history, a create or Restock only its Supply lists (a Restock adds no Withdrawal), never another Branch's (asserted in `back-office-cache.spec.ts`); a Salary Payment refreshes every payroll page and only the all-Branches expense report (payroll is studio-wide, BKO-001 Q3); an Expense refreshes its Branch's Expenses, that Branch's and the all-Branches report, and the category list (its «En uso»); a category change refreshes only the category list; the stock shown next to each Insumo lives in the option label, which is «next to the selected item» on every browser; the withdrawal quantity is checked against the stock last read and a server refusal re-reads it, the database keeping the last word (D4); the Idempotency-Key is kept per request body, so a retried lost answer replays and an edited body never reuses a key (`idempotency_key_reused`); Withdrawals and Restocks have no edit or delete anywhere (immutable, D6) and Salary Payments and Expenses only a correction (Q1); dates are no later than today in Bolivia on the client and the server (Q2); the Expense's Branch is shown as a line in its panel because AW14 has no field for it (D11); deleting an unused category asks first because it cannot be undone; the AW31 month picker is a stepper instead of a native month input, which Safari and Firefox do not draw; the history link carries `?page=1&pageSize=20` because TanStack Router's types require search for the nested route. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained panel actions and links instead of a second list layout for AW08; `DataTable`, `SidePanel`, `ConfirmDialog`, `ListFilter`, `StatusPill`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, the form primitives, `wholeNumberField`, `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`/`formatCivilMoment`, and the sidebar badge are reused; Horario's key predicate and no-Branch page were extracted at their second use; the Plan form's bolivianos field was extracted for the two Back Office amounts; one `CategoryAdder` serves AW14 and AW15; one problem mapper serves every Back Office code. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): may every Administrator see every Staff Member's pay in Nómina, or should payroll amounts be restricted or masked (payroll is personal data; BE-007)?; should corrections of a Salary Payment or Expense be readable somewhere (the audit records hold previous values, but no screen shows them)?; should a Withdrawal recorded by mistake get a void, since offsetting it with a Restock fixes the stock but leaves AW31's monthly consumption overstated (BKO-001 D6)? Gaps: Restocks have no read-back screen (AW31 lists Withdrawals only); native date pickers follow the browser's language (`mm/dd/yyyy` in an English Chrome), as in WEB-003; the Insumos count refreshes on this browser's changes and page loads, not live; the movement panels and AW31's filter read at most 100 Supplies per Branch; AW31 keeps a `supplyId` from another Branch after the selector changes (it shows the empty state); the bundle grew to 753 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; Back Office still has no unit-level specs (its adapters are integration-tested and the new transport parser is covered by e2e); AW16 (WEB-009) still has to combine `reports/income` and `reports/expenses`. Open for the user: follow-up 56 (with 33–40 and 48–55 still pending export) is ready to send to the design session. **Design follow-ups 48–51 exported 2026-09-30** (`43fd815`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas, read back and copied byte for byte after a render check against checklist 30 (its §6 now counts four AW08 actions); canvas deleted. AW08 lists «+ Nuevo insumo», «Registrar retiro», «Registrar reposición», «Ver historial de retiros» and draws the restock panel («Cantidad repuesta», whole units from 1); AW09's edit frame ends with «Archivar insumo» and its «¿Archivar este insumo?» dialog (Cancelar / Archivar, as 49 wrote; 59 §8 changes it to Volver); AW10 shows «Máximo: N unid.» and the «Solo hay N unid. en stock.» error with «Registrar» disabled; AW16 labels the payroll row «Nómina» («Viene de Nómina, no de las categorías») and adds a single-Branch frame (Centro) without it, with «Gastos registrados en Centro», «Pagos en efectivo cobrados en Centro», and the caption above the figures (59 §7 over 51 §2, as built). The shipped admin web already matches. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 56 is in the design system, AW08–AW15, AW31, and AW34; samples were adapted to Sucursal Equipetrol, AW11/AW12 sample Roles became Entrenadora/Asistente/Administración, and AW11–AW14 regained the Gastos and Categorías de gasto links checklist 30 §5 requires. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: **Evaluaciones nutricionales is Branch-scoped** (an Evaluation happens at a Branch, ADR-0001 and the glossary) while Referidos and Créditos stay studio-wide as `navigation.ts` marks them; AW25 reads a Bolivia week with Horario's stepper (the export already drew week arrows) rather than a free date range; the Nutricionista filter lists the Branch's currently assigned Nutritionists, deactivated included; the kind is only first/follow-up (the domain keeps no ordinal, so AW25's «Control · 3.ª evaluación» becomes «Seguimiento» as on S15); the narrow layout is one card per Evaluation like every other list instead of the export's day grouping; the Administrator Evaluation cancel (`DELETE /administrator/evaluations/{id}`) stays off the admin web because AW25 is read-only and invariant 10 allows no detail; Referral counts and the referrer total ignore the search, while the footer counts the matching rows; the Credit total block shows only without a search, because a searched page would not add up to it; «Última actividad» names all four ledger movements instead of the export's two; the URL keeps `q` like Clientes and Pagos and the query maps it to the backend's `query`. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained a panel-less mode and a footer slot instead of a second read-only layout; `WeekStepper` extracted at its second use; `staffOptionLabel` reused from Nómina; one `nameSearchSchema` serves AW26 and AW27; `DataTable`, `ListFilter`, `SearchFilter`, `StatusPill`, `NoBranchPage`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `formatBolivianos`, and `formatCivilDate` reused. Open questions for the user (data governance, not decided): should every Administrator see every Client's Credit balance and every Evaluation's Client name (Evaluation data handling is BE-007)?; should an Administrator be able to cancel an Evaluation from the admin web at all, given the route exists and AW25 is read-only? Gaps: a Nutritionist who lost the Branch assignment no longer appears in its filter, although her past Evaluations there stay under «Todas»; AW25 lists booked Evaluations only (cancelled ones never show, as ADM-001 D1 decided); Referral search and Credit search keep the ADM-001 cap of 80 characters and the backend's `query` name; the Credit list pages its balances in memory after one grouped query (fine for a studio's size, not for thousands of Clients); AW16 (WEB-009) is next. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 57 is in AW25–AW27. |

Expenses and Salary Payments already refreshed their report scopes since WEB-007); audit catalog `32160a3` (not audited); browser fixes `1073111` (the popover sits over the hovered month instead of the chart's centre) and `054a0d4` (Chromium's black calendar icon on every date field, WEB-003 and WEB-007 ones included, now a Texto tenue line icon); Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (es-BO, Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (Asia/Tokyo), after recording a Bs 350,00 Reparación Expense at Equipetrol for 15/07/2026 through the API: Todas (01/04–24/09/2026) showed Ingresos Bs 5.690,00 (Efectivo Bs 4.140,00 · Transferencias Bs 1.550,00), Gastos Bs 8.380,50 with Nómina Bs 7.100,00, Neto −Bs 2.690,50, bars only in July, August, and September, and September's popover «Ingresos Bs 4.910,00 / Gastos Bs 8.030,50»; Centro showed only its cash Bs 4.140,00 and its Bs 930,50 of Expenses, no Nómina row, the caption, and `?branch=` in the address; Equipetrol showed −Bs 350,00, and from 01/09 Bs 0,00 in all three; a backwards range kept the address and showed the alert; confirming Mariana Peña's pending Bs 560,00 transfer in Pagos raised Todas to Bs 6.250,00 (transfers Bs 2.110,00) without reloading while Centro stayed at Bs 4.140,00; at 420 px the tiles stacked, the chart kept its pairs, both tables became cards, the top-bar note was gone, no horizontal scroll, no console errors besides the pre-login `401` probe. Fixed from the walk: `1073111`, `054a0d4`. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (the schema refuses deleting Expenses and Payment decisions are final): the Equipetrol Expense of Bs 350,00 (with its `expense.recorded` audit row) and Mariana Peña's confirmed transfer (her Paquete de 8 clases started, with `payment.transfer_confirmed`). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: the report opens on «Todas las sucursales» (the one view the shell cannot give) and the last six Bolivia months up to today; its Branch selector lists archived Branches too, since their history still counts; Branch and range live in the address, and the shell's Branch choice neither drives nor follows it; the range is two native date fields (inclusive Bolivia dates) sent as Bolivia-midnight instants, `to` exclusive by a day; the report queries keep no placeholder data, so a scope switch shows the loading line instead of the previous scope's figures under the new label; amounts carry centavos everywhere (the export's «Bs 281.200» drops them; ADR-0082 and the design system write `Bs 1.050,00`); the net is signed «−Bs» in Plomo cálido; the Todas Ingresos tile splits cash and transfers (Credit-only Payments are Bs 0 by definition, so they are not shown); the payroll row is «Nómina» as follow-up 51 names it; the chart is HTML/CSS bars rather than a chart library (no dependency is justified for two series), and because Éxito and Error are close for deuteranopes (the dataviz validator: chroma floor FAIL for Éxito, deutan ΔE 6,6) it carries a legend, a 2 px gap between each pair, a per-month popover, and the month table; the closed palette is kept rather than changing the prescribed colours. Reuse decisions: `ListFilter`, `Input`, `PageHeader`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, the `Table` primitives with their card reflow, `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`, `formatMonth`, `addMonths`, and `boliviaInstant` reused; the report's route lives in `app/report-routes.tsx` beside WEB-008's route files. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): should income count Payments by the day they were confirmed (today, as ADM-001 decided) or by the day the transfer was sent?; should the report subtract Credit applied to Payments or show it as a separate line (income today is the amount the Client paid, net of Credit)?; may every Administrator see total payroll in «Todas las sucursales» (BKO-001 Q3, BE-007)? Gaps: the report has no export (CSV/PDF) and no comparison with a previous period; a very long range draws one narrow pair per month (no yearly grouping); native date pickers and the select arrow follow the browser (the select's arrow is still the browser's); the bundle grew to 779 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010. **Design follow-ups 48–51 exported 2026-09-30** (`43fd815`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas, read back and copied byte for byte after a render check against checklist 30 (its §6 now counts four AW08 actions); canvas deleted. AW08 lists «+ Nuevo insumo», «Registrar retiro», «Registrar reposición», «Ver historial de retiros» and draws the restock panel («Cantidad repuesta», whole units from 1); AW09's edit frame ends with «Archivar insumo» and its «¿Archivar este insumo?» dialog (Cancelar / Archivar, as 49 wrote; 59 §8 changes it to Volver); AW10 shows «Máximo: N unid.» and the «Solo hay N unid. en stock.» error with «Registrar» disabled; AW16 labels the payroll row «Nómina» («Viene de Nómina, no de las categorías») and adds a single-Branch frame (Centro) without it, with «Gastos registrados en Centro», «Pagos en efectivo cobrados en Centro», and the caption above the figures (59 §7 over 51 §2, as built). The shipped admin web already matches. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 58 is in AW16; its figures use the new «Cifra» step (Lora 500 · 32/1.2 · tabular) that 59 §10 adds. |
