Specs load every page chunk when `render-admin.tsx` loads (`preloadAdminPages`, `d2e90ab`) so a chunk's first compilation never lands inside a spec's first lookup. Coverage gaps closed by TDD: AW31 keeping a `supplyId` from another Branch (WEB-007 gap) and AW25 keeping a `nutritionistStaffMemberId` the new Branch does not offer, red `5c73ede` → `1ade604` (new `components/table/use-known-option.ts`: once a filter's options are known and the address's value is not among them, the filter is forgotten and the page returns to its first page; shared by both pages at the second use); the native select arrow painting the browser's black outside the closed palette (WEB-009 gap), red `6326808` → `2e2428c` (`select-chevron` utility in `index.css` beside the date icon: `appearance: none` and the same line chevron as the Branch cell in Texto tenue, with `pr-9` on the shared `Select` so `px-3` never wins over it; it reaches every `ListFilter` and every form select). Coverage-only specs: Horario's «Se muestran las primeras 100 sesiones» cap notice `d64d7ff`, Tipos de clase retrying a failed read `de52219`, Planes after a 403 hiding «Nuevo plan» `ded76b3`, and the sidebar showing no count when the Pagos or Insumos read fails `6b9ab93`. Playwright (ADR-0043): dependency `64ee791` (`@playwright/test` 1.61.0, whose Chromium is the cached build 1228; the `playwright` package has no build script, so `pnpm-workspace.yaml` is unchanged), config `32f5246` (`playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/support/{environment,global-setup,session}.ts`, `tsconfig.e2e.json` as a third project reference, an ESLint override with Node globals for `e2e/**`, `test:e2e` and the Prettier globs in the app, root `pnpm test:admin-e2e` that builds the backend and the admin web first, `test-results`/`playwright-report` ignored), journeys `bc19ccb` (Login to Horario with the host-only HttpOnly Secure Lax cookie unreadable from scripts, logout from the menu, a signed-out visit returning to `/catalogo/planes?page=2` after Login, a session ended behind the browser's back sending the next click to Login with «Tu sesión terminó» and back to Nómina afterwards), `2f5fd5a` (the Branch selector scoping Horario: Centro's Mat Pilates against Equipetrol's Pilates Prenatal on next week, the week staying in the address), `84d299c` (Personal's `role=trainer` in the address, surviving a reload), `c21f085` (an Expense of Bs 450,50 recorded at Centro with «Mantenimiento» typed into the inline category row, then Equipetrol still empty), `02810d8` (Camila the Assistant refused neutrally; a cookie request from `https://attacker.example` refused `403 untrusted_origin` while the session stays valid), `a5bafe8` (420 px: drawer, bottom-sheet selector, one card per member, no sideways scroll). The suite's global setup creates `pilates_test_<hex>` on the `pilates_test_fixtures` admin database through `psql`, runs `prisma migrate deploy` and `development-fixtures-main.js` with `DATABASE_URL` set explicitly (the backend's `.env` is never read for the database), starts `node dist/src/main.js` on 3101 with `ADMIN_WEB_TRUSTED_ORIGINS=http://localhost:4173` and `vite preview` on 4173 (whose proxy inherits `server.proxy`), waits for `/health/ready`, and returns a teardown that stops both by pid and drops the database; nine journeys in about 15 s, Chromium only, one worker, locale es-BO in a Europe/Madrid clock. Verified 2026-09-24: backend unit 99/100 suites, 456/457 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`); e2e 27/27 suites, 147/147 tests with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures`; integration 53/53 suites, 216/216 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, no leftover `pilates_test_*` databases; `pnpm check:api` clean, `check:api-compat` 2/2; admin web Jest 49 suites/330 tests (+1 suite, +7 tests) green twice in parallel and once in band, after `e519f95` raised Testing Library's lookup timeout to three seconds (a cold parallel worker's first whole-app render, in the two slowest suites Jest schedules first, kept outlasting the one-second default; assertions are unchanged); `eslint .`, `tsc -b` (app, specs, and e2e), Prettier (src, e2e, config), and `vite build` clean; Playwright 9/9 through `pnpm test:admin-e2e`, its database dropped and ports 3101/4173 free afterwards. Browser walk in the desktop app's built-in Chromium against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) with `vite preview` on 4173 (the split build) and `pnpm dev:admin` on 5173, as Paola, at 1440 px and 420 px: every route (Horario, the three catalog pages, the eight Back Office pages with Retiros, Reporte, Promociones, Referidos, Créditos, and `/no-existe`) loaded its own chunk on the preview build with no console error besides the pre-login `401` probe; the Visibilidad, Categoría, and Insumo selects and the Nuevo gasto panel's select drew the tenue chevron; choosing «Toallas de papel» in Retiros and switching to Equipetrol emptied the Insumo filter and the address (`?month=2026-09&page=1&pageSize=20`); at 420 px no page scrolled sideways, the drawer, the bottom-sheet selector, the card rows, the stacked report tiles, and the day tabs behaved as before; Gastos on the dev server at 420 px loaded with an empty console. The walk created nothing (reads and selector changes only), so nothing was deleted; earlier walks' rows (Toallas de papel, Cera para piso, Emma's two Salary Payments, the Equipetrol Expense) are still visible. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: lazy components sit on the route's `component` (not inside a wrapper) so intent preload fetches the chunk before the click, and the wrapper modules use `getRouteApi` by id rather than importing the route object, which would be a circular import; no `pendingComponent` was added, so the main area is blank for the milliseconds a cold chunk takes (the sidebar hover preload covers the usual case), recorded as a gap; the sign-in pages stay in the entry because they are the first thing a visitor needs; `preloadAdminPages` is exported from `router.tsx` because it is also what a browser would call to warm the pages after Login; a foreign filter is forgotten by an effect once the options are known (and page 1 restored), never by rendering a select whose value has no option; the select keeps its native element (ADR-0040 prefers semantic HTML) and only its arrow changes; Playwright journeys start each from a fresh sign-in and share one database in file order, so the Expense journey runs on the empty Gastos the fixtures leave; the session-expiry journey revokes the session through `page.request` (the browser's own cookie jar) because the 30-minute idle expiry cannot be waited for, and it ends in the same `401 authentication_required` path; the authorization journey is the Assistant's neutral refusal plus the `untrusted_origin` refusal, because the fixtures hold one Administrator, a second one cannot be activated without the email capture, and Paola cannot demote herself, so the page's 403 state stays component-tested (every list spec covers it); Playwright's `webServer` was not used because it starts before `globalSetup` and the database name is only known there; the teardown is the function `globalSetup` returns; the e2e backend runs on 3101 so a development backend on 3100 is never disturbed; only Chromium is configured because it is the one browser cached (the config names the `playwright install` step for the others). Reuse decisions: `useKnownOption` was extracted at its second use (AW31 and AW25) into `components/table` beside the other filter helpers; the chevron reuses the date icon's approach in `index.css` rather than a Radix Select; the e2e sign-in helper and fixture accounts live in `e2e/support` and are not shared with the Jest fixtures (different runtimes). Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): none new; WEB-004, WEB-005, WEB-007, WEB-008, and WEB-009's questions stand. Gaps, still open in this row: the Vercel preview, the real proxy (forwarded address, `trust proxy`, preview-origin registration), and the production deployment need EXT-003's accounts; Promociones landed in WEB-006 (its Playwright journey makes the suite ten); CI does not run any of these suites (EXT-003); Firefox and WebKit journeys need `playwright install`; a run killed before its teardown leaves its backend, preview, and database behind (Test 31 says how to find them); the blank main area while a cold chunk loads has no pending line; native date pickers still follow the browser's language; gaps that need backend work stay where their rows left them (Restock read-back, cancelled Sessions in Horario, invitation email correction, «Log out all other devices»). |

Kept as design-specified sizes with no token, not touched: the 10 px/0.18em AW34 group label, the 12.5 px README subtitle, the 15 px Lora day numerals, the 11 px avatar initials in Pagos, and the 32 px report figure (follow-up 59 §10). Cleanup: Andrea's Branches restored to Equipetrol through curl, the «Prueba WEB-011» Branch deleted from `booking.branches` (its two `staff_member.branches_changed` audit records stay, ids only), all three servers stopped by pid and the ports confirmed free. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: Horario is remounted per Branch (the keying fix) rather than dropping `keepPreviousData`, so stepping weeks inside one Branch still keeps the previous week on screen; a stale archived Branch is dropped silently on save instead of refused with a message, because the chips cannot show it and the backend would refuse the whole save; the guard resets the expiry on every authenticated `beforeLoad` (a no-op while a session lives); the search field trusts the address only when it differs from what it last sent; the report's unknown Branch falls back to «all» (its only neutral value) while list filters fall back to none; the wordmark moves to the top bar only when the sidebar is hidden (narrow), because design/README.md draws it in the 236 px cell and AW34 frame 4 gives the narrow top bar no cell; `rounded-sm` swatches became dots (`rounded-full`), since the scale's smallest corner (8 px) would round a 10 px square anyway; the 11.5 px pill and 12 px caption and initials took `text-ayuda` (the closest token, 11.5 px) while the 10 px label, 12.5 px subtitle, 15 px numerals, 11 px initials, and 32 px figure stay bracketed as design-specified sizes (the guard spec names only the four sizes with a token); focus after «Cerrar sesión» goes to «Cancelar» (the least destructive control, as Radix's AlertDialog does) and after «Rechazar» to the reason (the next step), returning to the row or button on cancel; the Branch selector keeps its listbox roles with a roving tabindex instead of becoming plain buttons, so the e2e journeys and specs keep `option`/`aria-selected`; `FormControl` learns of a description through a layout effect on `FormItem`'s context, at the cost of one extra render per field with helper text; the harness spec lives in `e2e/support/*.test.ts` under Jest (Playwright matches `*.spec.ts` only) because the helper is Node code the app's `src` project cannot type-check; `stopChild` never waits past the grace period, even if the child ignores SIGKILL's exit event; stderr goes to files by default and to the terminal under `E2E_DEBUG` so a normal run stays quiet. Reuse decisions: `everyBranchesQueryOptions` replaced two copies of the same read in Personal and a third with a different key in Reporte, so the three now share one cache entry; `namesById` and `archivedLast` extracted at their second use into `lib/named.ts`; `closeButtonClass` extracted from the receipt viewer at its second use; `useKnownOption` reused unchanged for three more filters; `useDocumentTitle` is one hook for the shell and the auth pages. ## Updating this backlog

When starting work, change one item to `in progress`. When completing it, change
it to `done` and replace Evidence with the commit hash. If implementation exposes
a missing product rule, mark the item `blocked`, record the exact question in the
row or owning context, and resolve it before continuing that behavior.
